closeout

closeout.goal.md, run with artie run from http://localhost:8392/.

The script: closeout.goal.md 
---
start: http://localhost:8392/
inputs:
  owner:     { question: "Whose repository?" }
  repo:      { question: "Which repository?" }
  version:   { question: "Which release ships the fixes?" }
  label:     { default: ready-to-close, question: "Which label marks an issue ready to close?" }
  shipped:   { default: shipped, question: "Which label means its fix shipped?" }
  wontfix:   { default: wontfix, question: "Which label means it won't be done?" }
  duplicate: { default: duplicate, question: "Which label means it repeats another issue?" }
---

Close each issue that is ready, the way its labels say it ended. Keep watching until the watch's issue is closed.

- In {owner}/{repo}
  - Open an issue titled "Close-out watch", saying "Close this issue to stop the watch." `as watch`
  - Whenever an issue labelled {label} changes `as issue`
    - If it's closed, leave it.
    - If it's labelled {shipped}, comment "Fixed in {version}."
    - If it's labelled {shipped}, close it as completed.
    - If it's labelled {wontfix}, close it as not planned.
    - If it's labelled {duplicate}, close it as a duplicate.
- Until someone closes {watch}

---
run:
  inputs:
    owner:
      value: JogoShugh
      given: given
      question: Whose repository?
    repo:
      value: heart-github-test
      given: given
      question: Which repository?
    version:
      value: '1.0'
      given: given
      question: Which release ships the fixes?
    label:
      value: ready-to-close
      given: default
      default: ready-to-close
      question: Which label marks an issue ready to close?
    shipped:
      value: shipped
      given: default
      default: shipped
      question: Which label means its fix shipped?
    wontfix:
      value: wontfix
      given: default
      default: wontfix
      question: Which label means it won't be done?
    duplicate:
      value: duplicate
      given: default
      default: duplicate
      question: Which label means it repeats another issue?
  source: |-
    act(intent = "get the repository $owner/$repo")
    act(intent = """open an issue titled "Close-out watch" with body "Close this issue to stop the watch."""")
  marks:
    '1':
      fromIntent: In ${owner}/${repo}
    '2':
      as: watch
      intent: Open an issue titled "Close-out watch", saying "Close this issue to stop the watch."
  moves:
    - 'why: Step is to open the repository by its names; the start offers repos-get.'
    - 'get the repository JogoShugh/heart-github-test: acted github:repos-get, done, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test'
    - 'why: The repository is already open, so this step that only names a place is complete.'
    - 'done: In JogoShugh/heart-github-test'
    - 'why: Open the watch issue with the given title and body using the open-issue form.'
    - 'open an issue titled "Close-out watch" with body "Close this issue to stop the watch.": acted github:open-issue, new resource, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2296'
    - 'why: The act opened the issue "Close-out watch" (#2296) with the requested body, so the step is complete.'
    - 'done: Open an issue titled "Close-out watch", saying "Close this issue to stop the watch."'
    - 'why: The repository offers no issue list link or form, so return to the start to find a form that lists issues filtered by label.'
    - back to the start, at http://localhost:8392/
    - 'why: The start offers issues-list-for-repo, which can filter by label; narrow the list to issues labelled $label.'
    - 'list issues for repo JogoShugh/heart-github-test filtered by label ready-to-close: decided github:issues-list-for-repo, at http://localhost:8392/'
    - 'why: The held issue 2292 must carry the ready-to-close label for the watch condition to pass.'
    - 'whenever: an issue labelled ready-to-close changes: decided the check {"github:labels":["ready-to-close"]}, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2292'
    - 'why: The if-condition is that the issue is closed, so check that its state is closed; it fails on this open issue.'
    - 'if: it''s closed: decided the check {"state":"closed"}, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2292'
    - 'why: The condition is that the issue is labelled shipped, so check that its labels include shipped; it should fail on this issue.'
    - 'if: it''s labelled shipped: decided the check {"github:labels":["shipped"]}, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2292'
    - 'why: The condition is that the issue is labelled shipped; check that its labels include shipped, which should fail on this issue (labels: duplicate, ready-to-close).'
    - 'if: it''s labelled shipped: decided the check {"github:labels":["shipped"]}, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2292'
    - 'why: The condition is that the issue is labelled wontfix, so check that its labels include wontfix; it should fail on this issue (labels: duplicate, ready-to-close).'
    - 'if: it''s labelled wontfix: decided the check {"github:labels":["wontfix"]}, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2292'
    - 'why: The condition is that the issue is labelled duplicate; check its labels include duplicate, which holds for this issue.'
    - 'if: it''s labelled duplicate: decided the check {"github:labels":["duplicate"]}, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2292'
    - 'why: Closing as duplicate needs the id of the original (the README issue about Java 22), which I don''t hold; go to the start to find it.'
    - 'back: refused, here only act, check, done, stop'
    - 'why: The issue is labelled duplicate, so close it as a duplicate using the close-duplicate form; its body says it duplicates the README issue about Java 22.'
    - 'close it as a duplicate (of the README issue about Java 22, as its body says): decided github:close-duplicate, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2292'
    - 'why: The close-duplicate act was answered and issue 2292 now shows state closed with reason duplicate, so this step is done.'
    - 'why: Issue 2291 is labelled wontfix, so close it as not planned with the close-not-planned form.'
    - 'close it as not planned: decided github:close-not-planned, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2291'
    - 'why: Issue 2291 is now closed with reason not_planned, so the close-as-not-planned step is done.'
    - 'why: Issue 2290 is labelled shipped, so comment the fix version on it.'
    - 'comment on it: Fixed in 1.0.: decided github:comment, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2290'
    - 'why: The comment "Fixed in 1.0." was posted on issue 2290 (comments now 1), so this step is complete.'
    - 'why: Issue 2290 is labelled shipped, so close it as completed with the close form.'
    - 'close it as completed: decided github:close, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2290'
    - 'why: Issue 2290 is shipped and now closed with state_reason completed, so this step is done.'
    - 'why: The watch ends once issue 2296 is closed, so check that its state is closed.'
    - 'until: someone closes http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2296: decided the check {"state":"closed"}, at http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2296'
    - 'watched: Whenever an issue labelled ready-to-close changes, until someone closes http://localhost:8392/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/issues/2296'
  model:
    name: claude-sonnet-5-5
    url: https://api.anthropic.com
  usage:
    calls: 22
    input: 53029
    output: 5042
    cache_read: 39154
    cache_write: 0
  watch:
    from: 5
    script: |-
      watch(until = { at("watch") { "state" eq "closed" } }) {
          act("github:issues-list-for-repo", "owner" to owner, "repo" to repo, "labels" to label, inferred = "list issues for repo ${owner}/${repo} filtered by label ${label}", fromIntent = "an issue labelled ${label} changes") {
              whenever {
                  has { "github:labels" has label }
                  leaveWhere("leave it.") { "state" eq "closed" }
                  given { "github:labels" has shipped } then {
                      act("github:comment", "body" to "Fixed in ${version}.", intent = """comment "Fixed in ${version}."""") {}
                  }
                  given { "github:labels" has shipped } then {
                      act("github:close", intent = "close it as completed.") {}
                  }
                  given { "github:labels" has wontfix } then {
                      act("github:close-not-planned", intent = "close it as not planned.") {}
                  }
                  given { "github:labels" has duplicate } then {
                      act("github:close-duplicate", intent = "close it as a duplicate.") {}
                  }
              }
          }
      }
    steps:
      - watch:
          until:
            - at: watch
              field: state
              is: closed
          steps:
            - act: github:issues-list-for-repo
              fields:
                owner: ${owner}
                repo: ${repo}
                labels: ${label}
              inferred: list issues for repo ${owner}/${repo} filtered by label ${label}
              from-intent: an issue labelled ${label} changes
              then:
                - whenever:
                    - has:
                        - field: github:labels
                          has: ${label}
                    - leave-where:
                        say: leave it.
                        facts:
                          - field: state
                            is: closed
                    - given:
                        - field: github:labels
                          has: ${shipped}
                      then:
                        - act: github:comment
                          fields:
                            body: Fixed in ${version}.
                          intent: comment "Fixed in ${version}."
                          then: []
                    - given:
                        - field: github:labels
                          has: ${shipped}
                      then:
                        - act: github:close
                          intent: close it as completed.
                          then: []
                    - given:
                        - field: github:labels
                          has: ${wontfix}
                      then:
                        - act: github:close-not-planned
                          intent: close it as not planned.
                          then: []
                    - given:
                        - field: github:labels
                          has: ${duplicate}
                      then:
                        - act: github:close-duplicate
                          intent: close it as a duplicate.
                          then: []
---
